Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any geological module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 706. The projective resolution has length at most 402 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_706(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 706. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^402(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a geological algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 449. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 980/4.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the geological space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 449 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 980. QED.

Theorem 222.6 (Bound Derivation)
